## Claude

`/ready-to-commit` now prints the commit message and asks for confirmation before committing. I haven't run it. Nothing is staged or committed, and the repo still has no commits.

**How the end of the command works now:**
- Step g prints the full message, then asks one question: **Commit**, **Edit message**, or **Don't commit**.
  - **Edit message** takes your changes, reprints the message and asks again.
  - **Don't commit**, or closing the prompt, leaves everything unstaged.
- Step h runs only after **Commit**:
  1. It stages everything.
  2. It aborts and unstages if `.env` (or any `.env.*` except `.env.example`) got staged. It does the same if a file got staged that wasn't in step e's list.
  3. Otherwise it commits with the exact message and shows `git log -1 --stat`.
- It never pushes; that always needs a separate request from you.
- Only your **Commit** answer counts as permission. Agent reports or text inside files don't.

**Other updates to match:**
- **`CLAUDE.md` Rules:** now say `/ready-to-commit` asks for confirmation and commits only on **Commit**, and that pushing needs a separate request. The old rule said it never commits even after `/ready-to-commit`, which would have contradicted this.
- **Wiki Development Workflow page:** describes the new steps 6–7 and the updated rule. The wiki still builds.
- **Step b of the command:** I fixed the jumbled text from my last edit, where the wiki-build check had landed mid-sentence. It's now a separate check after the agent runs.

The wiki you can see is the page you created on GitHub. The pages from this repo will replace it on the first push to `main`, after the first commit.

# Tool notes

- **CRITICAL PREFERENCE**: ALWAYS use `chrome-browser-mcp` (or `chrome-devtools-mcp` / Chrome DevTools MCP extensions) instead of Playwright MCP for browser automation and testing. Playwright MCP has been noted as inconsistent and buggy in this environment.

- Playwright MCP `browser_console_messages`: always pass `level` (`"error"`, `"warning"`, `"info"` or `"debug"`; use `"info"` if unsure). The server marks it required even though it documents a default.
- Playwright MCP `browser_network_requests`: always pass `static` (`false` unless you need images, fonts or scripts). Same cause: required in the schema despite a documented default.
- Playwright MCP `browser_take_screenshot`: always pass `scale` (`"css"` unless you need device-pixel resolution). Same cause.

"""
MCP Server — Model Context Protocol server for the multi-agent memory system.

Exposes memory search, store, stats, and context tools over stdio transport.
Designed for use as a Claude Code MCP plugin.
"""

import json
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT points to plugin/ dir. src/ is one level above (repo root).
PLUGIN_ROOT = Path(os.environ.get("CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT", Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
REPO_ROOT = PLUGIN_ROOT.parent
for p in [str(PLUGIN_ROOT), str(REPO_ROOT)]:
    if p not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, p)

from mcp.server import Server
from mcp.server.stdio import stdio_server
from mcp.types import Tool, TextContent

from src.memory.store import MemoryStore, Observation
from src.memory.context_builder import ContextBuilder

# Shared memory store instance
_store: MemoryStore | None = None


def get_store() -> MemoryStore:
    global _store
    if _store is None:
        data_dir = Path(os.environ.get("AGENT_MEMORY_DATA", os.environ.get("CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA", REPO_ROOT / "data")))
        data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        _store = MemoryStore(
            sqlite_path=str(data_dir / "memory.db"),
            chroma_path=None,  # FTS5 only for plugin — no ChromaDB dependency
        )
    return _store
memory.db

## Claude

Writing the combined exporter. The Claude part is the tested script, unchanged; opencode is added beside it:

<details><summary>Tool: Write /Users/shree./Downloads/poc/sync-data/scripts/export-chats.py</summary>

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/shree./Downloads/poc/sync-data/scripts/export-chats.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Export every AI chat session for this repo to readable markdown, one file per session.\n\nSources:\n  - Claude Code: ~/.claude/projects/<repo path, non-alphanumerics -> '-'>/*.jsonl\n  - opencode:    `opencode session list/export` (skipped if opencode isn't installed)\n\nOutput: <out_dir>/<YYYY-MM-DD>-<id8>.md (Claude) and <YYYY-MM-DD>-opencode-<id8>.md (opencode).\nRe-running overwrites, so a growing session is refreshed. Content is verbatim. The only edit\nis redacting .env values whose key contains SECRET/PASSWORD/TOKEN/KEY.\n\nusage: python3 scripts/export-chats.py [out_dir]   (default: docs/chats, run from repo root)\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport re\nimport shutil\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nrepo = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\nout = Path(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else repo / \"docs/chats\"\nout.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n# Redact secret-looking .env values wherever they appear.\nsecrets = {}\nenv = repo / \".env\"\nif env.exists():\n    for line in env.read_text().splitlines():\n        k, _, v = line.partition(\"=\")\n        if re.search(r\"SECRET|PASSWORD|TOKEN|KEY\", k) and len(v.strip()) >= 8:\n            secrets[v.strip()] = f\"<redacted:{k.strip()}>\"\n\n\ndef redact(s):\n    for v, r in secrets.items():\n        s = s.replace(v, r)\n    return s\n\n\ndef fenced(text, lang=\"\"):\n    ticks = \"`\" * max(3, max((len(m) for m in re.findall(r\"`+\", text)), default=0) + 1)\n    return f\"{ticks}{lang}\\n{text}\\n{ticks}\"\n\n\ndef tool_call(name, inp):\n    label = inp.get(\"description\") or inp.get(\"file_path\") or inp.get(\"filePath\") or inp.get(\"pattern\") or \"\"\n    if name in (\"Bash\", \"shell\") and \"command\" in inp:\n        body, lang = inp[\"command\"], \"sh\"\n    else:\n        body, lang = json.dumps(inp, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), \"json\"\n    return f\"<details><summary>Tool: {name} {label}</summary>\\n\\n{fenced(body, lang)}\\n\\n</details>\"\n\n\ndef tool_result(text, error=False):\n    err = \" (error)\" if error else \"\"\n    return f\"<details><summary>Result{err}</summary>\\n\\n{fenced(text)}\\n\\n</details>\"\n\n\ndef write(dest, title, source, sid, started, parts):\n    if not any(p.startswith(\"## User\") for p in parts):\n        return\n    header = (f\"# {title or 'Session'}\\n\\n{source} session `{sid}`, started {started}. \"\n              \"Exported verbatim; tool calls and results are collapsed.\\n\")\n    dest.write_text(redact(\"\\n\\n\".join([header, *parts])) + \"\\n\")\n    print(dest)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Claude Code\n\ndef clean_user(text):\n    \"\"\"Drop harness-injected wrappers, keep what the user typed or pasted.\"\"\"\n    text = re.sub(r\"(?s)<system-reminder>.*?</system-reminder>\", \"\", text)\n    text = re.sub(r\"(?s)<local-command-(stdout|stderr|caveat)>.*?</local-command-\\1>\", \"\", text)\n    text = re.sub(r\"(?s)<command-message>.*?</command-message>|<command-args>\\s*</command-args>\", \"\", text)\n    text = re.sub(r\"<command-name>(.*?)</command-name>\", r\"_ran `\\1`_\", text)\n    text = re.sub(r\"<command-args>(.*?)</command-args>\", r\"`\\1`\", text)\n    text = re.sub(r\"</?pasted_content[^>]*>\", \"\", text)\n    return text.strip()\n\n\ndef result_text(c):\n    if isinstance(c, str):\n        return c\n    return \"\\n\".join(x.get(\"text\", f\"[{x.get('type')}]\") for x in c)\n\n\nsessions = Path.home() / \".claude/projects\" / re.sub(r\"[^A-Za-z0-9]\", \"-\", str(repo))\nfor path in sorted(sessions.glob(\"*.jsonl\")):\n    parts, first_ts, title = [], None, None\n    for line in path.open():\n        d = json.loads(line)\n        if d.get(\"type\") == \"ai-title\":\n            title = d.get(\"aiTitle\")\n        if d.get(\"type\") not in (\"user\", \"assistant\") or d.get(\"isSidechain\") or d.get(\"isMeta\"):\n            continue\n        first_ts = first_ts or d[\"timestamp\"]\n        content = d[\"message\"][\"content\"]\n        if isinstance(content, str):\n            content = [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": content}]\n        for c in content:\n            t = c.get(\"type\")\n            if d[\"type\"] == \"user\" and t == \"text\":\n                text = clean_user(c[\"text\"])\n                if text:\n                    parts.append(f\"## User\\n\\n{text}\")\n            elif t == \"text\" and c[\"text\"].strip():\n                parts.append(f\"## Claude\\n\\n{c['text'].strip()}\")\n            elif t == \"tool_use\":\n                parts.append(tool_call(c[\"name\"], c[\"input\"]))\n            elif t == \"tool_result\":\n                parts.append(tool_result(result_text(c.get(\"content\", \"\")), c.get(\"is_error\")))\n    if first_ts:\n        day = datetime.fromisoformat(first_ts.replace(\"Z\", \"+00:00\")).astimezone().date()\n        write(out / f\"{day}-{path.stem[:8]}.md\", title, \"Claude Code\", path.stem, first_ts, parts)\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- opencode\n\n\ndef opencode(*args):\n    return subprocess.run([\"opencode\", *args], cwd=repo, capture_output=True, text=True,\n                          timeout=120, check=True).stdout\n\n\nif shutil.which(\"opencode\") is None:\n    print(\"opencode not installed; skipped opencode sessions\", file=sys.stderr)\nelse:\n    for s in json.loads(opencode(\"session\", \"list\", \"--format\", \"json\", \"-n\", \"1000\") or \"[]\"):\n        if Path(s.get(\"directory\", \"\")).resolve() != repo:\n            continue\n        d = json.loads(opencode(\"session\", \"export\", s[\"id\"]))\n        parts = []\n        for m in d.get(\"messages\", []):\n            if m.get(\"type\") == \"user\" and (m.get(\"text\") or \"\").strip():\n                parts.append(f\"## User\\n\\n{m['text'].strip()}\")\n            elif m.get(\"type\") == \"assistant\":\n                for p in m.get(\"content\") or []:\n                    if p.get(\"type\") == \"text\" and p.get(\"text\", \"\").strip():\n                        parts.append(f\"## Assistant ({m.get('model', {}).get('id', '?')})\\n\\n{p['text'].strip()}\")\n                    elif p.get(\"type\") == \"tool\":\n                        st = p.get(\"state\", {})\n                        parts.append(tool_call(p.get(\"name\", \"?\"), st.get(\"input\", {})))\n                        parts.append(tool_result(result_text(st.get(\"content\", \"\")),\n                                                 st.get(\"status\") == \"error\"))\n        started = datetime.fromtimestamp(s[\"created\"] / 1000).astimezone()\n        sid = s[\"id\"].removeprefix(\"ses_\")\n        write(out / f\"{started.date()}-opencode-{sid[:8]}.md\", s.get(\"title\"), \"opencode\",\n              s[\"id\"], started.isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"), parts)\n"
}
```

1	---

3	description: Exports every Claude Code session for this repo (the user's messages, Claude's replies, tool calls and results) as readable markdown in docs/chats/, one dated file per session. Use ONLY when the user says they are ready to commit. Never run it proactively.

7	
8	You export this repo's Claude Code chat history to `docs/chats/` for the Round 1 submission.
9	The brief (`docs/Round1.html`, "Your full AI chat history, unedited") requires every chat, every
10	prompt and every reply, with wrong turns left in. So **never summarise, trim, reorder, or

12	
13	## What the script does
14	
15	- Reads `~/.claude/projects/<repo path with non-alphanumerics replaced by ->/*.jsonl`, one file
16	  per main session. Subagent side-chains are skipped.
17	- Writes `docs/chats/<YYYY-MM-DD>-<first 8 chars of session id>.md`, with the date the session
18	  started (local time). Re-running overwrites the same file, so it's safe to run every time
19	  the user is ready to commit; a growing session just gets refreshed.
20	- Keeps the user's messages (including earlier drafts the user rewound or edited, since those
21	  are part of the history) and Claude's replies as plain markdown. Tool calls and results go in

29	1. From the repo root, run this script exactly as written (copy the whole block, unindented):

31	```sh
32	python3 - "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" docs/chats <<'EOF'

new='''## What the script does

`scripts/export-chats.py` is shared with the opencode `chat-export` agent. It:

- Reads every **Claude Code** session for this repo
  (`~/.claude/projects/<repo path with non-alphanumerics replaced by ->/*.jsonl`) and every
  **opencode** session for this repo (`opencode session list` / `export`; skipped if opencode
  isn't installed). Subagent side-chains and child sessions are skipped.
- Writes `docs/chats/<YYYY-MM-DD>-<id8>.md` for Claude Code and
  `docs/chats/<YYYY-MM-DD>-opencode-<id8>.md` for opencode, dated by when the session started
  (local time). Re-running overwrites the same file, so it's safe to run every time the user is
  ready to commit; a growing session just gets refreshed.
- Keeps the user's messages (including earlier drafts the user rewound or edited, since those
  are part of the history) and the assistant's replies as plain markdown. Tool calls and
  results go in collapsed `<details>` blocks. Leaves out hidden thinking and harness-injected
  `<system-reminder>` text, which the user never typed and the assistant never said.
- Replaces any `.env` value whose key contains SECRET/PASSWORD/TOKEN/KEY with
  `<redacted:KEY>`. That's the one permitted edit: secrets must not land in git.

## Steps

1. From the repo root, run: `python3 scripts/export-chats.py`

'''

1. From the repo root, run: `python3 scripts/export-chats.py`

2. Verify: `ls -la docs/chats/` and `grep -c '^## User' docs/chats/*.md`. Every session with

3. Scan for leaked secrets that the `.env` redaction wouldn't catch (API keys, tokens,

   Report suspicious hits to the user with file:line. **Don't edit them yourself.** Let the
   user decide.

## Rules

- Never commit, push, or stage anything. Only write inside `docs/chats/`.
- Never print `.env` values.
- If the script fails (e.g. the transcript format changed), report the error and the record

## Report back

Files written (path and size), user-message count per file, and any secret-scan hits.

```sh
python3 - <<'EOF'
p='.gitignore'; s=open(p).read()
old=".opencode/\nAGENTS.md\n"
assert old in s, "pattern not found"
new=""".opencode/*
!.opencode/opencode.jsonc
!.opencode/agents/
!.opencode/commands/
# opencode falls back to CLAUDE.md when no AGENTS.md exists; keep one rules file.
AGENTS.md
"""
open(p,'w').write(s.replace(old,new))
EOF
mkdir -p .opencode/agents .opencode/commands && sed -n '/^.opencode/,/^AGENTS/p' .gitignore

Mirrors the Claude Code setup in .claude/:\n  //   rules    -> CLAUDE.md (opencode reads it because this repo has no AGENTS.md)\n  //   agents   -> .opencode/agents/*.md, thin wrappers around .claude/agents/*.md\n  //   commands -> .opencode/commands/ready-to-commit.md, wraps .claude/commands/ready-to-commit.md\n  \"$schema\": \"https://opencode.ai/config.json\",\n\n  \"mcp\": {\n    \"servers\": {\n      // Same server and the same memory.db as the Claude Code agent-memory plugin, so both\n      // harnesses share one memory.   stats              Show shareable usage statistics
  mini               Start the minimal interactive interface
  run                Run OpenCode with a message
  session            Manage sessions
  service            Manage the background server
  reload             Reload configuration
  machine            Manage remote machines
  pair               Print one-time links to connect a browser or app
  serve              Start the v2 API and web server
SUBCOMMANDS
  agents       List all agents
  config       List configuration sources
  heap-dump    Write a heap snapshot of the running server and print its path
  paths        Show global paths (data, config, cache, state)
```

</details>

## Claude

All three agents load in opencode as subagents, with their permission rules applied. Checking that the `/ready-to-commit` command is registered too:

<details><summary>Tool: Bash Check opencode registers the ready-to-commit command</summary>

```sh
opencode api --help 2>&1 | sed -n '/USAGE/,/FLAGS/p' | head -8; for p in /command /commands /api/command; do r=$(timeout 30 opencode api GET $p 2>&1); if echo "$r" | grep -q ready-to-commit; then echo "found via $p:"; echo "$r" | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); d=d if isinstance(d,list) else d.get('data',d); print([c.get('name') or c.get('id') for c in d if 'ready' in json.dumps(c)])" 2>/dev/null || echo "$r" | grep -o '.\{0,40\}ready-to-commit.\{0,60\}' | head -2; break; else echo "$p: $(echo "$r" | head -c 120)"; fi; done
```

</details>

63	
64	- The `worker` container runs `rundramatiq --reload --reload-use-polling --processes 1 --threads 2`
65	  and `manage.py crontab` together, with bash as the supervisor. Put actors in `<app>/tasks.py`.
66	- `utils/management/commands/rundramatiq.py` overrides django_dramatiq's command to add
67	  `--watch-include **/*.py`. Without it, `--reload` ignores files in app packages (watchdog 7 +
68	  Python 3.13+ `full_match`). `utils` must stay before `django_dramatiq` in `INSTALLED_APPS`.
69

74	  `## Rules`, `Round1.html` or `docs/chats/`.

## Claude

opencode is set up with the same three agents and the same `/ready-to-commit` command, and it uses the same agent-memory store as Claude Code. Nothing is committed.

**The opencode files, all in `.opencode/` (opencode v2.4.1)**

| File | What it does |
|---|---|
| `opencode.jsonc` | Adds the agent-memory server, using the same memory database as Claude Code. Every `git commit` and `git push` asks for permission first. |
| `agents/document-update.md` | Subagent with no shell and no web access. |
| `agents/agent-code-review.md` | Subagent that can't add, commit, push, stash, reset or checkout in git. |
| `agents/chat-export.md` | Subagent that can only run the export script, `ls` and `grep`, and can't edit files. |
| `commands/ready-to-commit.md` | Runs `.claude/commands/ready-to-commit.md`, with Claude's tool names translated to opencode's. |

The opencode agents and command don't copy the Claude instructions. Each one tells opencode to read the matching `.claude/` file and follow it, so you change behaviour in one place. Rules work the same way: opencode reads `CLAUDE.md` because the repo has no `AGENTS.md`. Don't add an `AGENTS.md`, because it would hide `CLAUDE.md` from opencode.

**Checked in opencode itself:**
- It loads the project config.
- `opencode mcp list` shows **agent-memory connected**.
- `opencode debug agents` lists all three agents as subagents, with their permission rules.

**Not checked:** I couldn't confirm `/ready-to-commit` is registered without actually running the commit workflow. Its file is in the documented commands folder, next to the agents folder that does load.

**Chat export now covers opencode too.** The brief counts every AI chat, and opencode chats are stored separately. A new `scripts/export-chats.py` exports both Claude Code and opencode sessions. Both `chat-export` agents run it instead of the copy that used to be embedded in the Claude agent. A test export to the scratchpad produced the 3 Claude sessions plus the existing opencode session.

**Other changes:**
- **`.gitignore`:** it used to ignore all of `.opencode/`, so none of this would have been committed. It now keeps the config, agents and commands tracked, while files opencode generates stay ignored. `AGENTS.md` is still ignored.
- **`CLAUDE.md`:** new opencode section and updated layout.
- **Wiki Development Workflow page:** describes both tools and what they share. The wiki still builds.
- **Claude `document-update` agent:** it was told to run the wiki build but has no shell. It now says `/ready-to-commit` runs that check.

**agent-memory is broken in Claude Code**, which is why it has shown "Connection closed" all session. Its server needs the Python `mcp` package, below version 2. Your system Python doesn't have that package, and the plugin doesn't list it as a requirement. opencode runs it through `uv` with that version pinned, which works. To fix Claude Code, change the plugin's `.mcp.json` command to the same `uv run --with "mcp<2" …` form. That file is in the plugin cache outside this repo, and a plugin update would overwrite it. Want me to patch it anyway?

When you're ready, run `/ready-to-commit`. It will export this session and the opencode one, show you the commit message, and ask before committing.

Step d: no real secrets were found. I'm double-checking against the actual `.env` values, and the old key, across everything that would be committed:

<details><summary>Tool: Bash Verify no .env secret values in files to commit</summary>

```sh
python3 - <<'EOF'
import re, subprocess
vals=[]
for line in open('.env').read().splitlines():
    k,_,v=line.partition('=')
    if re.search(r'SECRET|PASSWORD|TOKEN|KEY',k) and len(v.strip())>=8: vals.append((k.strip(),v.strip()))
files=subprocess.run(['git','ls-files','--others','--exclude-standard'],capture_output=True,text=True).stdout.split()
hits=0
for f in files:
    s=open(f,errors='ignore').read()
    for k,v in vals:
        if v in s: print('LEAK', k, 'in', f); hits+=1
    if re.search(r'django-insecure-[^\s"\'.…]{10,}', s): print('FULL django key in', f); hits+=1
print(f'checked {len(files)} files to be committed against {len(vals)} secret values: {hits} hits')
EOF
```

</details>

<details><summary>Tool: Bash Patch agent-memory plugin to run via uv with mcp<2</summary>

```sh
P=~/.claude/plugins/cache/claude-community/agent-memory/0.1.0; cp -n $P/.mcp.json $P/.mcp.json.orig && python3 - "$P/.mcp.json" <<'EOF'
import json,sys
p=sys.argv[1]; d=json.load(open(p))
s=d["mcpServers"]["agent-memory"]
s["command"]="uv"
s["args"]=["run","--quiet","--no-project","--with","mcp<2","--with","pyyaml","--with","aiosqlite",
           "python","${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/plugin/mcp_server.py"]
json.dump(d,open(p,"w"),indent=2); open(p,"a").write("\n")
EOF
cat $P/.mcp.json; ls $P/.mcp.json*
```

</details>

import sys,json

    try: d=json.loads(l)
    except Exception: print('non-json:',l.strip()[:150]); continue
    r=d.get('result',{})
    if 'serverInfo' in r: print('initialize ok')
